# LoanOS Project Map

Tech stack: Next.js 14 App Router, Supabase, Tailwind, Vercel

## .
.DS_Store - macOS Finder metadata for the project folder.
.env.example - Template environment variables for local setup.
.env.local - Local environment variable overrides for development.
.env.local.example - Example local environment file for developer onboarding.
.eslintrc.json - ESLint configuration for the application codebase.
.gitignore - Git ignore rules for generated and local-only files.
2026-03-12_LoanOS-Automation-Audit.md - Project documentation for 03 12 loanos automation audit.
2026-03-16_LoanOS-Pitch-Deck.pptx - Presentation deck for 03 16 loanos pitch deck.
ARCHITECTURE.md - Project documentation for architecture.
CHANGELOG.md - Project documentation for changelog.
CLAUDE.md - Project documentation for claude.
CONTEXT.md - Project documentation for context.
LOANOS_SYSTEM_KNOWLEDGE_BASE.md - Project documentation for loanos system knowledge base.
README.md - Project documentation for readme.
RENOVATION-PLAN.md - Project documentation for renovation plan.
loanos-arive-n8n-prompt.md - Project documentation for loanos arive n8n prompt.
loanos-audit-prompt.md - Project documentation for loanos audit prompt.
loanos-deep-research.md - Project documentation for loanos deep research.
next-env.d.ts - Next.js generated TypeScript environment declarations.
next.config.mjs - Next.js runtime and build configuration.
package-lock.json - Locked dependency graph for reproducible NPM installs.
package.json - NPM manifest defining app scripts, dependencies, and metadata.
postcss.config.mjs - PostCSS configuration used by the Tailwind build pipeline.
tailwind.config.ts - Tailwind CSS theme and content scanning configuration.
tsconfig.json - Primary TypeScript compiler configuration for the project.
tsconfig.tsbuildinfo - Incremental TypeScript build metadata cache.
vitest.config.ts - Vitest test runner configuration for unit tests.
zapier_webhook_fields.md - Project documentation for zapier webhook fields.
